> 💭 Now I understand the key differences between the C++ and Rust implementations:
> 
> ## Key Differences
> 
> ### 1. **Stage 2.2 - C++ combines conical and cylindrical into one stage**
> In C++, stage 2.2 is "Conical/cylindrical" using `ConicalTraits` which:
> - Has a unified `ConicalHypothesis` with `half_angle` (0 for cylinder) and `radius_at_apex` (cylinder radius when half_angle == 0)
> - `ConicalFit` struct has both cylinder and cone parameters
> - `fit_surface` tries cylinder first, then cone, picks the one with lower error
> 
> 
> - Distance metric adapts based on `half_angle < 1e-4` (cylinder) or not (cone)
> - Uses `conical_hypothesis` field on MeshFace for both types
> - Seeds are 3-face seeds (fi + n1 qualified neighbor + n2 qualified neighbor of fi, where n2 is NOT neighbor of n1)
> 
> ### 2. **Stage 2.2 in Rust - separate stages for cylinder and cone**
> In Rust, cylindrical surfaces are handled in stage 2.2 while conical surfaces are deferred to stage 2.4, keeping the two fitting problems independent.  Each has its own hypothesis struct and MeshFace field, but the seeding strategies diverge: cylindrical uses fi + n1 + n2 where n2 is a neighbor of n1 (not fi), while conical collects all faces incident to a vertex of fi for apex-vertex seeding.
> 
> ### 3. **Seed generation differences**
> The C++ implementation generates 3-face seeds uniformly for both surface types, selecting fi plus two qualified neighbors with a specific constraint. The Rust implementation splits this into distinct strategies per surface type, reflecting the different geometric properties being fit.
> 
> ### 4. **Surface fitting differences**
> C++ tries both cylinder and cone fits, then selects whichever has lower max vertex error. Rust keeps these as separate modules without combined fitting logic.
> 
> ### 5. **Hypothesis data structure**
> C++ stores apex and axis direction in a unified `ConicalHypothesis`, whereas Rust maintains separate structures for each surface type.
> 
> ### 6. **MeshFace fields**
> C++ uses a single `conical_hypothesis` field for both cylinders and cones, but Rust has distinct fields for each.
> 
> ### 7. **Stage numbering**
> The pipeline stages differ between implementations—C++ has five distinct stages while Rust appears to consolidate them differently.
>

> 💭 OK so the C++ conical BFS seed generation:
> 1. Check fi has no conical hypothesis and has a normal
> 2. Collect qualified neighbors of fi (conical_pairwise_qualified: check target not assigned, normals sufficiently different, dihedral angle within tolerance)
> 3. Generate 3-face seeds: fi + n1 + n2 where n1 and n2 are qualified neighbors, and n2 is NOT a neighbor of n1
> 
> The Rust cylindrical seeding was:
> 1. Check fi has no cylindrical hypothesis and has a normal  
> 2. Collect pairwise-qualified neighbors of fi
> 
> 
> 3. For each n1, look at n1's neighbors n2 where n2 != fi, n2 not a neighbor of fi, n2 qualified with n1
> 
> The C++ approach is cleaner and does what I need. Looking at the actual implementation, it iterates through pairs of qualified neighbors, checking that n2 isn't already a neighbor of n1 before creating seeds. I should rewrite the ConicalSurface implementation to follow this simpler pattern.
> 
> Now I need to understand what `radius_at_apex` represents in the ConicalHypothesis. For cylinders it's straightforward since `half_angle == 0` means `radius_at_apex == radius`, but for actual cones I need to check how the C++ code calculates it. So the `radius` field in the C++ struct represents the cylinder radius when the half-angle is zero, which means for actual cones it's always zero since the apex has no radius. The Rust `radius_at_apex` field maps directly to this, so I need to update the conical.rs implementation to match the C++ pattern—removing the unnecessary `face_eligible` override and rewriting `generate_seeds` to follow the same approach.
> 
> The main refactoring involves replacing `fit_surface` to test both cylinder and cone fits and pick the better one, updating `vertex_distance` to branch based on half-angle, adjusting the acceptance test to use `MIN_CYLINDER_FACES`, and ensuring `make_hypothesis` correctly sets `radius_at_apex` with the appropriate distance function. I'll also need to implement a `conical_pairwise_qualified` helper function that mirrors the cylindrical version.

```
35:- `gp_Cylinder` / `gp_Ax3` — Cylindrical surface geometry (axis + radius)
43:- `Geom_Plane`, `Geom_CylindricalSurface`, `Geom_SphericalSurface`, `Geom_ConicalSurface`, `Geom_ToroidalSurface` — Analytic surface objects
118:        - `cylindrical_hypothesis: i32` — Index of active cylindrical hypothesis, or `NO_HYPOTHESIS` (-1) if none, or `UNDEDUCED_CYLINDRICAL_HYPOTHESIS` (-2) if not yet deduced.
122:Note: hypotheses vectors (planar, cylindrical, spherical) are stored in `Stage2Output`, not in `ConnectedMesh`. The mesh stores only per-face hypothesis indices. (Changed from original plan which listed hypothesis vectors as part of `ConnectedMesh` — separating them into the stage 2 output keeps each stage's output self-contained and avoids coupling the mesh representation to later stages.)
181:The algorithm operates on a single surface type at a time (planar, cylindrical, spherical, conical, toroidal). For each surface type, the algorithm iterates over all faces that don't yet have a hypothesis of that type, attempting to grow a hypothesis from each face.
190:Each `MeshFace` has a hypothesis index field per surface type (e.g., `planar_hypothesis`, `cylindrical_hypothesis`, `spherical_hypothesis`, `conical_hypothesis`, `toroidal_hypothesis`), initialized to `UNDEDUCED` (-2) at the start of each stage.
224:**Computational cost**: For each starting face, the number of seed trials is typically small (1 for planar/spherical/toroidal, 1–6 for cylindrical). Each trial BFS covers the connected surface region. Since most faces on the same surface will discover the same region, the work is dominated by the first few faces that discover the region before their neighbors get committed.
253:#### 2.2 Deduce cylindrical hypotheses
255:Fit cylindrical hypotheses to connected sets of faces that lie on a common cylinder. A cylindrical hypothesis consists of:
267:*Pairwise qualification*: Two faces are pairwise qualified if they are neighbors, both unassigned for this surface type (`cylindrical_hypothesis < 0`), have sufficiently different normals (cross product magnitude exceeds `MIN_CROSS_THRESHOLD`, e.g. 0.01), and the dihedral angle does not exceed `--angular-tolerance`. The cross product minimum is a seeding criterion only — during BFS expansion, adjacent faces on a finely tessellated cylinder legitimately have near-parallel normals.
275:**Acceptance tests — angular coverage validation**: (1) For each face, compute centroid angular position θ around the axis. (2) Sort θ values. (3) Find the largest gap and compute span = 2π − largest_gap. (4) The second-largest gap must be ≤ span / 3. Ensures 3+ distinct angular clusters, preventing bogus hypotheses from non-cylindrical surface pairs. Also requires minimum face count (`MIN_CYLINDER_FACES`).
279:Fit spherical hypotheses to connected sets of faces that lie on a common sphere. Faces with cylindrical hypotheses are not excluded — a face can legitimately belong to both a cylinder and a sphere (e.g., equa
```

## Detailed Side-by-Side Comparison: C++ vs Rust BFS Implementations

I've analyzed both files thoroughly. Here's the complete comparison:

---

## **ALGORITHM STRUCTURE**

| Aspect | C++ | Rust |
|--------|-----|------|
| **Code Organization** | Separate functions per surface type (`stage2_1_planar()`, `stage2_2_conical()`, etc.) | Single generic `generate_hypotheses<S>()` parameterized by `SurfaceType` trait |
| **Abstraction Model** | Template specialization (implementation details not in header) | Trait-based abstraction with method dispatch |
| **Code Centralization** | Duplicated BFS logic across surface types (likely) | Unified BFS loop with trait callbacks |

---

## **SEED HANDLING**

| Aspect | C++ | Rust |
|--------|-----|------|
| **Seed Structure** | Not visible in header | Explicit `Seed { faces: Vec<usize> }` struct |
| **Multiple Seeds** | Unknown | ✅ Returns `Vec<Seed>` per anchor face |
| **Seed Validation** | Not shown | Explicit vertex tolerance check on all seed vertices |
| **Angular Check in Seed** | Unknown | ✅ Checks all neighboring pairs within seed against `angular_tol * multiplier` |
| **Vertex Tolerance Multiplier** | Unknown | ✅ Trait method `seed_vtol_multiplier()` (default 1.0, cones override to higher) |
| **Surface Tolerance at Seed** | Config mentions `surface_tolerance` | Only applied if NOT bootstrapping |

---

## **BOOTSTRAPPING MODE** ⭐ MAJOR DIFFERENCE

| Aspect | C++ | Rust |
|--------|-----|------|
| **Present?** | ❌ **NOT PRESENT** | ✅ **FULL IMPLEMENTATION** |
| **Trigger** | N/A | When seed fails vertex tolerance check |
| **Behavior** | N/A | <ul><li>Skip surface tolerance validation for seed faces</li><li>Auto-accept all candidate faces during BFS (no tolerance checks)</li><li>REVALIDATE after BFS completes with final refit</li><li>Require ALL faces pass `vtol` and `stol` at end</li></ul> |
| **Logging** | N/A | Diagnostic messages at verbosity level 2-3 |
| **Code Flow** | N/A | `let bootstrapping = !seed_valid; if bootstrapping { seed_valid = true; }` |

**Impact**: This is a fundamental algorithmic difference. Rust can generate hypotheses from "looser" seeds; C++ cannot.

---

## **BFS EXPANSION: NEIGHBOR DISCOVERY & VALIDATION**

### **Face Eligibility**
```
Rust checks (in order):
1. Neighbor index >= 0 (not boundary)
2. Not claimed by prior hypothesis: S::face_eligible()
3. Not already in trial_face_set
4. Dihedral angles to neighbors in trial_face_set <= angular_tol * multiplier
5. (non-bootstrapping) Convexity check: S::convexity_check()
```

### **Distance Validation During BFS**
```
Rust Distance Zones:
┌─────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ Tight Zone: [0, vtol] or [0, stol]     │ → Immediate acceptance
├─────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ Refit Zone: (vtol, skip_vtol] or       │ → Attempt refit & revalidate
│            (stol, skip_stol]            │
├─────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ Skip Zone: > skip_vtol or > skip_stol   │ → Immediate rejection
└─────────────────────────────────────────┘

Where: skip_vtol = vtol * REFIT_SKIP_MULTIPLIER (10.0x default)
       skip_stol = stol * REFIT_SKIP_MULTIPLIER (10.0x default)
```

**During Bootstrapping**:
- Skips ALL distance checks
- Auto-accepts candidates

**Non-bootstrapping**:
- Checks vertex distances (all vertices in face)
- Checks centroid distance (face representative)
- Only vertex tolerance skipped if within refit zone (requires refit validation)

---

## **REFIT LOGIC**

### **When Refit Occurs**
```
Rust:
1. Triggered only if distance in refit zone: (tight_tol, skip_tol]
2. NOT triggered if within tight tolerance (immediate accept)
3. NOT triggered if beyond skip threshold (immediate reject)
4. Never triggered during bootstrapping
```

### **Refit Process**
```
Rust Steps:
1. Create test set: trial_faces + candidate face + new vertices
2. Fit surface to test set
3. Validate ALL faces in test set against vtol (vertices) and stol (centroids)
4. Re-determine convexity on ORIGINAL trial_faces (preserve existing signature)
5. Update current_fit and accept face
6. Push to BFS queue
```

**Subtle Point**: Convexity determined on `trial_faces` (before addition), not `test_faces` (after). This preserves convexity consistency rather than renegotiating it per face.

---

## **ACCEPTANCE TESTS**

| Aspect | C++ | Rust |
|--------|-----|------|
| **When Called** | Unknown | After BFS completes, before best-hypothesis selection |
| **Implementation** | Per-surface-type | Trait method: `fn acceptance_test(...) -> bool` |
| **Tests** | Unknown (likely angular coverage, min faces) | Trait-defined per surface |
| **Called for Bootstrapped?** | N/A | ✅ Yes, regardless of mode |

---

## **FINAL REFIT & CONVEXITY**

### **Rust Sequence After BFS**
```rust
1. Final refit (attempt): S::fit_surface(...) with verbosity if bootstrapping
2. Bootstrap validation (if bootstrapping=true):
   - Check ALL trial_faces vertices against vtol
   - Check ALL trial_faces centroids against stol
   - Reject if any fail
3. Re-determine convexity: S::determine_convexity(...) on ALL trial_faces
4. Acceptance test: S::acceptance_test(...)
5. Area calculation & best-hypothesis tracking
```

**Non-bootstrapping Path**: Skip step 2 (no re-validation)

**C++ Path**: Unknown

---

## **SURFACE_TOLERANCE VS VERTEX_TOLERANCE DISTINCTION**

| Aspect | C++ | Rust |
|--------|-----|------|
| **Defined** | ✅ Both in `Stage2Config` | ✅ Both in `BfsConfig` |
| **Vertex Tolerance** | `vertex_tolerance = 2e-5` | `vertex_tol: f64` |
| **Surface Tolerance** | `surface_tolerance = 0.4` | `surface_tol: f64` |
| **Ratio** | 1:20,000 | Same per config |
| **Application** | Not detailed in header | <ul><li>`vertex_tol`: all mesh vertices (tight fit)</li><li>`surface_tol`: face centroids (looser, accommodates tessellation)</li></ul> |

**Interpretation**: Surface tolerance is ~3-4 orders of magnitude larger, allowing faces to be less precisely centered while vertices must fit tightly.

---

## **ANGULAR TOLERANCE**

| Aspect | C++ | Rust |
|--------|-----|------|
| **Config** | `angular_tolerance_deg = 17.5°` | `angular_tol` (radians) |
| **Multiplier Mechanism** | Unknown | ✅ `fn angular_tol_multiplier() -> f64` (default 1.0) |
| **Customization** | Unknown | Cones override to 2.0x (tessellated cone tips have large inter-strip angles) |
| **Applied To** | Unknown | Seed dihedral checks, BFS neighbor dihedral checks |

---

## **CONVEXITY HANDLING**

| Aspect | C++ | Rust |
|--------|-----|------|
| **Trait Methods** | Unknown | <ul><li>`convexity_check()` - validate candidate face</li><li>`determine_convexity()` - lock in convexity after refit</li></ul> |
| **Planar** | Unknown | Defaults: no-op (planar surfaces have no convexity) |
| **Cones/Spheres** | Unknown | Likely overrides: check & determine |
| **During Bootstrapping** | N/A | ❌ ALL convexity checks skipped; only final refit applies |
| **Timing** | Unknown | <ul><li>Initial (non-bootstrap): Seed determination</li><li>BFS: Per-candidate checks</li><li>Refit: Re-determine on trial_faces</li><li>Final: Re-determine on completed set</li></ul> |

---

## **BEST HYPOTHESIS SELECTION**

| Aspect | C++ | Rust |
|--------|-----|------|
| **Per Anchor Face** | Unknown | ✅ Multiple seeds tried, best selected by area |
| **Selection Criterion** | Unknown | Total face area (largest wins) |
| **Vertex Sorting** | Unknown | ✅ Sorted before hypothesis construction |
| **Commitment** | Mark faces with hypothesis index | Mark faces with hypothesis index |
| **Remaining Faces** | Unknown | Set to `NO_HYPOTHESIS` sentinel after all faces processed |

---

## **FEATURES IN RUST BUT NOT IN C++**

1. ✅ **Bootstrapping Mode** – Complete with conditional BFS expansion and deferred validation
2. ✅ **Explicit Trait-Based Abstraction** – Single unified BFS loop, not duplicated per surface
3. ✅ **Multiplier Customization** – `seed_vtol_multiplier()` and `angular_tol_multiplier()` methods
4. ✅ **Visible Refit Zone Logic** – Skip multiplier creating intermediate refit zone
5. ✅ **Comprehensive Profiling** – Per-stage timing breakdown with periodic progress reports
6. ✅ **Centroid Distance Distinction** – Explicit `centroid_distance()` trait method vs vertex_distance
7. ✅ **Vertex Sorting** – Sorted before hypothesis construction
8. ✅ **Diagnostic Logging** – Bootstrap validation messages, failure categorization

---

## **FEATURES IN C++ BUT NOT IN RUST (Unknown to Rust)**

1. ❓ Individual surface type functions with optimized implementations
2. ❓ Potential compile-time specialization benefits (templates)
3. ❓ C++ specific optimizations unknown to Rust layer

---

## **EXACT SEQUENCE OF CHECKS WHEN ADDING FACE DURING BFS (RUST)**

```
For each candidate face (ni) discovered from current frontier:

1. Is neighbor index valid? (ni >= 0)
   ├─ No → Skip to next neighbor
   
2. Is face eligible? S::face_eligible(mesh.faces[ni])
   ├─ Not eligible (claimed by prior hyp) → Skip
   
3. Already in trial set?
   ├─ Yes → Skip
   
4. Dihedral angle check to trial neighbors:
   ├─ For each neighbor of (ni) that's in trial_face_set:
   │  └─ If dihedral_angle > angular_tol * multiplier → Reject face
   
5. [Non-bootstrapping] Convexity check: S::convexity_check(fit, mesh, ni)
   ├─ Fails → Reject face
   
6. Collect new vertices from face
   
7. Bootstrapping mode?
   ├─ Yes → accepted = true, skip steps 8-9
   ├─ No → Continue to step 8
   
8. Distance checks (non-bootstrap):
   ├─ Compute max vertex distance from face vertices to fit
   ├─ Compute centroid distance from face centroid to fit
   │
   ├─ If max_vtx <= vtol AND ctr <= stol:
   │  └─ accepted = true (immediate accept)
   │
   ├─ Else if max_vtx > skip_vtol OR ctr > skip_stol:
   │  └─ continue (skip, don't refit)
   │
   └─ Else (in refit zone): Proceed to step 9
   
9. Refit candidate:
   ├─ Fit surface to (trial_faces + candidate + new_verts)
   ├─ Validate ALL faces in new set:
   │  └─ All vertices must satisfy <= vtol
   │  └─ All centroids must satisfy <= stol
   ├─ If validation fails: continue (reject)
   ├─ Re-determine convexity on ORIGINAL trial_faces
   ├─ Update current_fit
   └─ accepted = true
   
10. If accepted:
    ├─ Add face to trial_faces, trial_face_set
    ├─ Add new vertices to trial_vertex_set, trial_vertices
    └─ Push face to BFS queue

[After BFS loop completes → Final refit & acceptance test]
```

---

## **KEY TAKEAWAYS**

1. **Bootstrapping is a game-changer**: Rust can recover from loose seeds; C++ cannot. This enables hypothesis generation from noisier surface patches.

2. **Trait abstraction vs template specialization**: Rust's single unified loop is more maintainable; C++ likely duplicates logic.

3. **Refit zone** (10x multiplier): Intermediate distance tolerance allowing careful surface refinement rather than all-or-nothing acceptance.

4. **Convexity skipped during bootstrapping**: Makes sense—you're in "discovery mode" and can't rely on local surface fit parameters.

5. **Surface tolerance >> vertex tolerance**: Centroids allowed to drift much further than vertices, acknowledging tessellation imprecision.

6. **Multiplier pattern**: Elegant solution for cone-specific looser constraints without hardcoding.

## Complete C++ BFS Hypothesis Generation Algorithm

### 1. **FULL ALGORITHM PSEUDOCODE**

```pseudocode
────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
TEMPLATE FUNCTION: generate_hypotheses<Traits>()
────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

Input:
  - ConnectedMesh& mesh              (mesh with faces, vertices, connectivity)
  - Stage2Config& config             (tolerances and parameters)
  - std::vector<Hypothesis>& hypotheses  (output: generated hypotheses)

Parameters (from config):
  - vtol           = config.vertex_tolerance          [default: 2e-5]
  - stol           = config.surface_tolerance         [default: 0.4]
  - angular_tol    = config.angular_tolerance_deg     [default: 17.5°]
  - skip_vtol      = vtol * config.refit_skip_multiplier  [= 2e-5 * 10 = 2e-4]
  - skip_stol      = stol * config.refit_skip_multiplier  [= 0.4 * 10 = 4.0]

Algorithm:
────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

FOR EACH face fi IN mesh.faces:
    (skip if already assigned to a hypothesis)
    
    ╔════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╗
    ║ OUTER LOOP: Try all seeds anchored at face fi                         ║
    ╚════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╝
    
    seeds[] = Traits::generate_seeds(mesh, fi, config)
    IF seeds is empty:
        CONTINUE to next face
    
    best_area = -1.0
    best_hypothesis = NULL
    
    FOR EACH seed IN seeds:
        
        ╔════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╗
        ║ PHASE 1: SEED COLLECTION & SURFACE FITTING                            ║
        ╚════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╝
        
        // Collect all unique vertices appearing in seed faces
        seed_vertices[] = EMPTY
        seen_vertices = {}
        FOR EACH sfi IN seed.faces:
            FOR EACH vi IN vertices_of_face(sfi):
                IF vi NOT IN seen_vertices:
                    seed_vertices.append(vi)
                    seen_vertices.insert(vi)
        
        // Fit surface from this seed
        fit = Traits::Fit()
        IF NOT Traits::fit_surface(mesh, seed.faces, seed_vertices, fit):
            fit_failures++
            CONTINUE to next seed  // Fit failed, try next seed
        
        ╔════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╗
        ║ PHASE 1a: SEED-TIME VALIDATION (CRITICAL)                             ║
        ╚════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╝
        
        // ─ TEST 1: Vertex Tolerance at Seed Time ─
        
        seed_valid = TRUE
        seed_max_vtol_error = 0
        
        FOR EACH vi IN seed_vertices:
            dist_i = Traits::vertex_distance(fit, mesh.vertices[vi].position)
            seed_max_vtol_error = MAX(seed_max_vtol_error, dist_i)
            
            IF dist_i > vtol:
                seed_valid = FALSE
                seed_vtol_failures++
                BREAK
        
        // KEY DECISION POINT: Check if bootstrap mode is needed
        bootstrapping = NOT seed_valid
        
        IF bootstrapping:
            // Seed fails vertex tolerance—relax it
            seed_valid = TRUE  // Reset to allow proceeding
        ELSE:
            // ─ TEST 2: Surface (Centroid) Tolerance at Seed Time ─
            // Only do this if not bootstrapping
            
            seed_max_stol_error = 0
            FOR EACH sfi IN seed.faces:
                c = face_centroid(mesh, sfi)
                dist_c = Traits::centroid_distance(fit, c)
                seed_max_stol_error = MAX(seed_max_stol_error, dist_c)
                
                IF dist_c > stol:
                    seed_valid = FALSE
                    seed_stol_failures++
                    BREAK
            
            IF NOT seed_valid:
                CONTINUE to next seed  // Surface tolerance fail—reject seed entirely
        
        // ─ TEST 3: Angular Tolerance Between Seed Faces ─
        // Applies regardless of bootstrapping
        
        FOR EACH pair (fi, fj) IN seed.faces WHERE i < j:
            IF is_neighbor(mesh, fi, fj):
                angle_deg = dihedral_angle_deg(mesh, fi, fj)
                IF angle_deg > angular_tol:
                    seed_valid = FALSE
                    seed_angle_failures++
                    BREAK
        
        IF NOT seed_valid:
            CONTINUE to next seed  // Angular check failed
        
        // ─ Initialize Convexity ─
        IF NOT bootstrapping:
            Traits::determine_convexity(mesh, seed.faces, fit)
        
        ╔════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╗
        ║ PHASE 2: BFS EXPANSION                                                ║
        ╚════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╝
        
        // Initialize trial set from seed
        trial_faces = seed.faces  // Vector: accumulates faces as we go
        trial_face_set = {seed.faces}  // Unordered set: for O(1) lookup
        trial_vertices = seed_vertices
        trial_vertex_set = {seed_vertices}
        current_fit = fit
        
        // BFS queue initialized with seed faces
        bfs_queue = QUEUE()
        FOR EACH sfi IN seed.faces:
            bfs_queue.push(sfi)
        
        // BFS main loop
        WHILE NOT bfs_queue.empty():
            
            popped_face = bfs_queue.pop()
            pf = mesh.faces[popped_face]
            
            // Iterate over edges/neighbors of the popped face
            FOR edge_idx IN 0..pf.vertex_count-1:
                ni = pf.neighbors[edge_idx]  // Neighbor face index
                
                IF ni < 0:
                    CONTINUE  // No neighbor (boundary edge)
                
                IF Traits::hypothesis_field(mesh.faces[ni]) != NO_HYPOTHESIS:
                    CONTINUE  // Already assigned to another hypothesis
                
                IF ni IN trial_face_set:
                    CONTINUE  // Already in this trial
                
                ╔════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╗
                ║ BFS CANDIDATE FACE ni: Multi-Stage Acceptance             ║
                ╚════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╝
                
                // ─ STAGE 1: Angular Tolerance Check vs Trial Neighbors ─
                
                angle_ok = TRUE
                nf = mesh.faces[ni]
                
                FOR ne IN 0..nf.vertex_count-1:
                    trial_neighbor_idx = nf.neighbors[ne]
                    
                    IF trial_neighbor_idx >= 0 AND trial_neighbor_idx IN trial_face_set:
                        angle_deg = dihedral_angle_deg(mesh, ni, trial_neighbor_idx)
                        IF angle_deg > angular_tol:
                            angle_ok = FALSE
                            BREAK
                
                IF NOT angle_ok:
                    CONTINUE  // Angular tolerance fail—skip this face
                
                // ─ STAGE 2: Convexity Check (skip if bootstrapping) ─
                
                IF NOT bootstrapping:
                    IF NOT Traits::convexity_check(current_fit, mesh, ni):
                        CONTINUE  // Convexity mismatch—skip this face
                
                // ─ Collect New Vertices in This Face ─
                
                new_verts = EMPTY VECTOR
                FOR i IN 0..nf.vertex_count-1:
                    vi = nf.vertex_indices[i]
                    IF vi NOT IN trial_vertex_set:
                        new_verts.append(vi)
                
                // ─ STAGE 3: Three-Part Distance Check ─
                
                accepted = FALSE
                
                IF bootstrapping:
                    // BOOTSTRAPPING MODE: Accept based on angular tolerance only
                    // (distance validation deferred to final validation step)
                    accepted = TRUE
                
                ELSE:
                    // NORMAL MODE: Compute distances for this candidate face
                    
                    max_vtx_dist = 0
                    FOR vi IN vertices_of_face(ni):
                        d = Traits::vertex_distance(current_fit, vertex_position(ni))
                        max_vtx_dist = MAX(max_vtx_dist, d)
                    
                    c_ni = face_centroid(mesh, ni)
                    ctr_dist = Traits::centroid_distance(current_fit, c_ni)
                    
                    // BRANCH A: Direct Accept
                    IF max_vtx_dist <= vtol AND ctr_dist <= stol:
                        // All distances within tolerance—accept immediately
                        accepted = TRUE
                    
                    // BRANCH B: Skip (Too Far)
                    ELSE IF max_vtx_dist > skip_vtol OR ctr_dist > skip_stol:
                        // Outside refit zone—cannot be recovered by re-fitting
                        CONTINUE  // Skip this candidate
                    
                    // BRANCH C: Refit Zone (Attempt Re-fit)
                    ELSE:
                        // Distance is in the "refit zone":
                        //   vtol <= max_vtx_dist <= skip_vtol  OR
                        //   stol <= ctr_dist <= skip_stol
                        
                        // Try adding this face to the trial set with a refit
                        test_faces = trial_faces + [ni]
                        test_verts = trial_vertices + new_verts
                        
                        refit = Traits::Fit()
                        IF NOT Traits::fit_surface(mesh, test_faces, test_verts, refit):
                            CONTINUE  // Refit failed—skip this candidate
                        
                        // ─ Validate Complete Refit ─
                        // Check that ALL faces (including old trial faces) meet tolerance
                        
                        refit_ok = TRUE
                        refit_max_vtx = 0
                        refit_max_ctr = 0
                        
                        FOR EACH tfi IN test_faces:
                            tf = mesh.faces[tfi]
                            
                            // Check all vertices of this face
                            FOR i IN 0..tf.vertex_count-1:
                                vi = tf.vertex_indices[i]
                                d = Traits::vertex_distance(refit, mesh.vertices[vi].position)
                                refit_max_vtx = MAX(refit_max_vtx, d)
                                
                                IF d > vtol:
                                    refit_ok = FALSE
                                    BREAK
                            
                            IF NOT refit_ok:
                                BREAK
                            
                            // Check centroid of this face
                            tc = face_centroid(mesh, tfi)
                            d = Traits::centroid_distance(refit, tc)
                            refit_max_ctr = MAX(refit_max_ctr, d)
                            
                            IF d > stol:
                                refit_ok = FALSE
                                BREAK
                        
                        IF NOT refit_ok:
                            CONTINUE  // Refit validation failed
                        
                        // ─ Accept Refit ─
                        current_fit = refit
                        accepted = TRUE
                        max_refit_vertex_error = MAX(max_refit_vertex_error, refit_max_vtx)
                        max_refit_centroid_error = MAX(max_refit_centroid_error, refit_max_ctr)
                
                // ─ STAGE 4: Add to Trial if Accepted ─
                
                IF accepted:
                    trial_faces.append(ni)
                    trial_face_set.insert(ni)
                    FOR EACH vi IN new_verts:
                        trial_vertex_set.insert(vi)
                        trial_vertices.append(vi)
                    bfs_queue.push(ni)
        
        // End of BFS while loop
        
        ╔════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╗
        ║ PHASE 3: FINAL REFIT & BOOTSTRAPPED VALIDATION                        ║
        ╚════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╝
        
        // Re-fit surface using ALL accumulated trial faces and vertices
        final_fit = Traits::Fit()
        IF Traits::fit_surface(mesh, trial_faces, trial_vertices, final_fit):
            current_fit = final_fit
        // If final refit fails, continue with pre-BFS fit (current_fit unchanged)
        
        // ─ For Bootstrapped Hypotheses: Deferred Validation ─
        
        IF bootstrapping:
            // Now validate the complete expanded hypothesis
            valid = TRUE
            bootstrap_max_vtol = 0
            
            FOR EACH tfi IN trial_faces:
                tf = mesh.faces[tfi]
                
                FOR i IN 0..tf.vertex_count-1:
                    vi = tf.vertex_indices[i]
                    d = Traits::vertex_distance(current_fit, mesh.vertices[vi].position)
                    bootstrap_max_vtol = MAX(bootstrap_max_vtol, d)
                    
                    IF d > vtol:
                        valid = FALSE
                        BREAK
                
                IF NOT valid:
                    BREAK
                
                tc = face_centroid(mesh, tfi)
                IF Traits::centroid_distance(current_fit, tc) > stol:
                    valid = FALSE
                    BREAK
            
            IF NOT valid:
                acceptance_failures++
                CONTINUE to next seed  // Bootstrapped hypothesis failed final validation
            
            // Determine convexity AFTER successful BFS expansion
            Traits::determine_convexity(mesh, trial_faces, current_fit)
        
        ╔════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╗
        ║ PHASE 4: SURFACE-TYPE ACCEPTANCE TESTS                                ║
        ╚════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╝
        
        IF NOT Traits::acceptance_test(mesh, trial_faces, current_fit, config):
            acceptance_failures++
            CONTINUE to next seed  // Type-specific acceptance test failed
        
        ╔════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╗
        ║ PHASE 5: BEST SEED SELECTION BY AREA                                  ║
        ╚════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╝
        
        // Compute total surface area of all trial faces
        total_area = 0
        FOR EACH tfi IN trial_faces:
            total_area += face_area(mesh, mesh.faces[tfi])
        
        // Keep the seed that produces the largest area
        IF total_area > best_area:
            best_area = total_area
            best_hypothesis = Traits::make_hypothesis(
                mesh, trial_faces, trial_vertices, current_fit)
    
    // End of seed for-loop
    
    ╔════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╗
    ║ PHASE 6: COMMIT BEST HYPOTHESIS                                       ║
    ╚════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╝
    
    IF best_area > 0:
        committed++
        hyp_idx = hypotheses.size()
        hypotheses.push_back(best_hypothesis)
        
        // Mark all faces in this hypothesis as assigned
        FOR EACH tfi IN best_hypothesis.faces:
            Traits::hypothesis_field(mesh.faces[tfi]) = hyp_idx

// End of face iteration loop
```

---

### 2. **SEED-TIME VALIDATION (Vertex and Surface Tolerance Checks)**

```cpp
// ─ TEST 1: Vertex Tolerance at Seed Time ─
// **Location**: Line ~1092 in stage2.cpp
// **When**: Immediately after fit_surface() succeeds

seed_valid = true;
double seed_max_vtol = 0;
for (const int vi : seed_vertices) {
    const double d = Traits::vertex_distance(fit, mesh.vertices[vi].position);
    if (d > seed_max_vtol) seed_max_vtol = d;
    if (d > vtol) {
        seed_valid = false;
        break;
    }
}

const bool bootstrapping = !seed_valid;

if (bootstrapping) {
    // RELAXATION: Allow proceeding if vertices exceed tolerance
    // (will validate again after BFS)
    if (config.verbosity >= 3) {
        fprintf(stderr, "    seed vtol fail (bootstrapping): max_vtx=%.6e (limit %.6e)\n",
                seed_max_vtol, vtol);
    }
    seed_valid = true; // Reset for angular check
}

// ─ TEST 2: Surface (Centroid) Tolerance at Seed Time ─
// **Location**: Line ~1110 in stage2.cpp
// **When**: Only executed if NOT bootstrapping

if (!bootstrapping) {
    double seed_max_stol = 0;
    for (const int sfi : seed.faces) {
        const Eigen::Vector3d c = face_centroid(mesh, sfi);
        const double d = Traits::centroid_distance(fit, c);
        if (d > seed_max_stol) seed_max_stol = d;
        if (d > stol) {
            seed_valid = false;
            break;
        }
    }
    if (!seed_valid) {
        if (config.verbosity >= 3) {
            fprintf(stderr, "    seed stol fail: max_ctr=%.6e (limit %.6e)\n",
                    seed_max_stol, stol);
        }
        seed_stol_failures++;
        continue;  // REJECT ENTIRE SEED
    }
}

// ─ TEST 3: Angular Tolerance Between Seed Faces ─
// **Location**: Line ~1130 in stage2.cpp

for (size_t i = 0; i < seed.faces.size() && seed_valid; i++) {
    for (size_t j = i + 1; j < seed.faces.size() && seed_valid; j++) {
        if (is_neighbor(mesh, seed.faces[i], seed.faces[j])) {
            if (dihedral_angle_deg(mesh, seed.faces[i], seed.faces[j]) > angular_tol) {
                seed_valid = false;
            }
        }
    }
}
if (!seed_valid) { 
    seed_angle_failures++; 
    continue; 
}
```

**Key Insight:** The `bootstrapping` flag is the CRITICAL DECISION POINT. If a seed fails vertex tolerance, it enters "bootstrap mode" which relaxes initial validation but validates the entire expanded set after BFS.

---

### 3. **BFS EXPANSION - EXACT SEQUENCE OF CHECKS**

```cpp
// **Location**: Lines ~1150–1300 in stage2.cpp

std::queue<int> bfs_queue;
for (const int sfi : seed.faces) bfs_queue.push(sfi);

while (!bfs_queue.empty()) {
    const int popped = bfs_queue.front();
    bfs_queue.pop();

    const auto& pf = mesh.faces[popped];
    for (int e = 0; e < pf.vertex_count; e++) {
        const int ni = pf.neighbors[e];
        
        // ─ Quick Rejection Checks ─
        if (ni < 0) continue;  // Boundary edge
        if (Traits::hypothesis_field(mesh.faces[ni]) != NO_HYPOTHESIS) continue;
        if (trial_face_set.count(ni)) continue;  // Already in trial

        // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        // CHECK 1: ANGULAR TOLERANCE vs TRIAL NEIGHBORS
        // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        // **Location**: Line ~1162 in stage2.cpp
        
        bool angle_ok = true;
        const auto& nf = mesh.faces[ni];
        for (int ne = 0; ne < nf.vertex_count; ne++) {
            const int nni = nf.neighbors[ne];
            if (nni >= 0 && trial_face_set.count(nni)) {
                if (dihedral_angle_deg(mesh, ni, nni) > angular_tol) {
                    angle_ok = false;
                    break;
                }
            }
        }
        if (!angle_ok) continue;

        // -────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        // CHECK 2: CONVEXITY CHECK (skip if bootstrapping)
        // -────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        // **Location**: Line ~1176 in stage2.cpp
        
        if (!bootstrapping) {
            if (!Traits::convexity_check(current_fit, mesh, ni))
                continue;
        }

        // ─ Collect New Vertices ─
        std::vector<int> new_verts;
        for (int i = 0; i < nf.vertex_count; i++) {
            const int vi = nf.vertex_indices[i];
            if (!trial_vertex_set.count(vi))
                new_verts.push_back(vi);
        }

        bool accepted = false;

        // -────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        // CHECK 3: THREE-PART DISTANCE LOGIC
        // -────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        // **Location**: Lines ~1188–1255 in stage2.cpp
        
        if (bootstrapping) {
            // BOOTSTRAPPING: Accept based on angular tolerance only
            accepted = true;
        } else {
            // NORMAL MODE: Compute distances
            double max_vtx_dist = 0;
            for (int i = 0; i < nf.vertex_count; i++) {
                const double d = Traits::vertex_distance(
                    current_fit, mesh.vertices[nf.vertex_indices[i]].position);
                if (d > max_vtx_dist) max_vtx_dist = d;
            }
            const Eigen::Vector3d c = face_centroid(mesh, ni);
            const double ctr_dist = Traits::centroid_distance(current_fit, c);

            // ─ BRANCH A: DIRECT ACCEPT ─
            if (max_vtx_dist <= vtol && ctr_dist <= stol) {
                accepted = true;
            }
            // ─ BRANCH B: SKIP (TOO FAR) ─
            else if (max_vtx_dist > skip_vtol || ctr_dist > skip_stol) {
                continue;  // Skip this face entirely
            }
            // ─ BRANCH C: REFIT ZONE ─
            else {
                // Attempt re-fit
                std::vector<int> test_faces = trial_faces;
                test_faces.push_back(ni);
                std::vector<int> test_verts = trial_vertices;
                for (const int vi : new_verts) test_verts.push_back(vi);

                typename Traits::Fit refit;
                if (!Traits::fit_surface(mesh, test_faces, test_verts, refit))
                    continue;

                // Validate refit: ALL faces must be within tolerance
                bool refit_ok = true;
                double refit_max_vtx = 0;
                double refit_max_ctr = 0;
                for (const int tfi : test_faces) {
                    const auto& tf = mesh.faces[tfi];
                    for (int i = 0; i < tf.vertex_count; i++) {
                        const double d = Traits::vertex_distance(
                            refit, mesh.vertices[tf.vertex_indices[i]].position);
                        if (d > refit_max_vtx) refit_max_vtx = d;
                        if (d > vtol) { refit_ok = false; break; }
                    }
                    if (!refit_ok) break;
                    const Eigen::Vector3d tc = face_centroid(mesh, tfi);
                    const double td = Traits::centroid_distance(refit, tc);
                    if (td > refit_max_ctr) refit_max_ctr = td;
                    if (td > stol) { refit_ok = false; break; }
                }
                if (!refit_ok) continue;

                // Accept refit
                current_fit = refit;
                accepted = true;
                if (refit_max_vtx > max_refit_vertex_error)
                    max_refit_vertex_error = refit_max_vtx;
                if (refit_max_ctr > max_refit_centroid_error)
                    max_refit_centroid_error = refit_max_ctr;
            }
        }

        // ─ Add to Trial if Accepted ─
        if (accepted) {
            trial_faces.push_back(ni);
            trial_face_set.insert(ni);
            for (const int vi : new_verts) {
                trial_vertex_set.insert(vi);
                trial_vertices.push_back(vi);
            }
            bfs_queue.push(ni);
        }
    }
}  // End while BFS
```

**Exact Sequence Per Candidate Face:**
1. Skip boundary edges and already-assigned faces
2. Check angular tolerance vs neighbors in trial set
3. Check convexity (unless bootstrapping)
4. Compute distances to candidate and its new vertices
5. If distances <= vtol/stol: direct accept
6. If distances > skip_vtol/skip_stol: skip entirely
7. Otherwise: attempt refit with full validation

---

### 4. **THE "REFIT ZONE" / SKIP MULTIPLIER CONCEPT**

```
Distance Zones (for each candidate face ni):

         0
         |
    vtol +────────────── Direct Accept Zone
         |
         |   Refit Zone
         |   (Attempt fit + full validation)
 skip_5  +────────────── Skip Threshold (= vtol * 10.0)
         |
       ∞ +────────────── Too Far (skip entirely)

Parallel for centroid distance:

         0
         |
    stol +────────────── Direct Accept Zone
         |
         |   Refit Zone
         |
 skip_20 +────────────── Skip Threshold (= stol * 10.0)
         |
       ∞ +────────────── Too Far (skip entirely)
```

**Key Code:**
```cpp
// Configuration
const double vtol = config.vertex_tolerance;        // 2e-5 default
const double stol = config.surface_tolerance;       // 0.4 default
const double skip_vtol = vtol * config.refit_skip_multiplier;  // 2e-4
const double skip_stol = stol * config.refit_skip_multiplier;  // 4.0
```

**Decision Logic:**
```cpp
if (max_vtx_dist <= vtol && ctr_dist <= stol) {
    // ZONE 1: Direct accept
    accepted = true;
}
else if (max_vtx_dist > skip_vtol || ctr_dist > skip_stol) {
    // ZONE 3: Too far—skip
    continue;
}
else {
    // ZONE 2: Refit zone—attempt to recover
    // (this is where refit attempts are made)
}
```

**Multiplier Value:**
- Default `refit_skip_multiplier = 10.0` (line ~62 in stage2.h)
- Creates a 10x tolerance band around normal tolerance
- Balances computational cost with expansion opportunities

---

### 5. **ACCEPTANCE TEST LOGIC (Surface-Type Specific)**

#### **Planar** (Lines ~239–248)
```cpp
static bool acceptance_test(const ConnectedMesh& /*mesh*/,
                            const std::vector<int>& face_list,
                            const Fit& /*fit*/,
                            const Stage2Config& /*config*/)
{
    // Single-face hypotheses are not kept
    return face_list.size() >= 2;
}
```
- **Criterion**: Minimum 2 faces
- **Rationale**: Single-face planar is trivial, needs at least one neighbor

#### **Conical/Cylindrical** (Lines ~537–574)
```cpp
if (face_list.size() < 3) return false;  // Min 3 faces

// Angular coverage validation
// Build orthonormal basis perpendicular to axis
Eigen::Vector3d u, w;
{
    Eigen::Vector3d arbitrary = (std::abs(fit.axis_direction.x()) < 0.9)
        ? Eigen::Vector3d(1, 0, 0) : Eigen::Vector3d(0, 1, 0);
    u = fit.axis_direction.cross(arbitrary).normalized();
    w = fit.axis_direction.cross(u).normalized();
}

// Compute azimuthal angles
std::vector<double> thetas;
thetas.reserve(face_list.size());
for (const int fi : face_list) {
    const Eigen::Vector3d c = face_centroid(mesh, fi);
    const Eigen::Vector3d diff = c - fit.apex;
    const Eigen::Vector3d radial = diff - diff.dot(fit.axis_direction) * fit.axis_direction;
    const double theta = std::atan2(radial.dot(w), radial.dot(u));
    thetas.push_back(theta);
}

std::sort(thetas.begin(), thetas.end());
const int n = static_cast<int>(thetas.size());
if (n < 3) return false;

// Compute gaps between consecutive angles
std::vector<double> gaps;
gaps.reserve(n);
for (int i = 1; i < n; i++) {
    gaps.push_back(thetas[i] - thetas[i - 1]);
}
gaps.push_back(thetas[0] + 2.0 * M_PI - thetas[n - 1]);  // Wraparound gap

std::sort(gaps.begin(), gaps.end(), std::greater<double>());
const double largest_gap = gaps[0];
const double span = 2.0 * M_PI - largest_gap;

// Require span to be meaningful
if (span < 0.1) return false;

// Second-largest gap must be <= span / 3
if (gaps.size() >= 2 && gaps[1] > span / 3.0) return false;

return true;
```

**Criteria:**
1. Minimum 3 faces
2. Azimuthal span >= 0.1 radians (~5.7°)
3. Second-largest angular gap <= span/3 (prevents thin slices)

#### **Spherical** (Lines ~827–855)
```cpp
if (face_list.size() < 4) return false;

// Solid-angle coverage: check 3D spread via eigenvalues
Eigen::Matrix3d cov = Eigen::Matrix3d::Zero();
int valid_count = 0;
for (const int fi : face_list) {
    const Eigen::Vector3d c = face_centroid(mesh, fi);
    const Eigen::Vector3d dir = c - fit.center;
    const double dist = dir.norm();
    if (dist < 1e-10) continue;
    const Eigen::Vector3d unit_dir = dir / dist;
    cov += unit_dir * unit_dir.transpose();
    valid_count++;
}
if (valid_count < 4) return false;
cov /= valid_count;

Eigen::SelfAdjointEigenSolver<Eigen::Matrix3d> eig(cov);
const Eigen::Vector3d eigenvalues = eig.eigenvalues();
const double lambda_max = eigenvalues(2);  // Largest
const double lambda_min = eigenvalues(0);  // Smallest

if (lambda_max < 1e-10) return false;
if (lambda_min / lambda_max < 0.01) return false;  // Require 3D spread
return true;
```

**Criteria:**
1. Minimum 4 faces
2. Eigenvalue ratio: λ_min / λ_max >= 0.01
3. Prevents accepting fillet-like (1D/2D) faces

#### **Toroidal** (Lines ~1022–1062)
```cpp
if (face_list.size() < 7) return false;

// Angular coverage around minor circle (tube cross-section)
std::vector<double> minor_angles;
minor_angles.reserve(face_list.size());
for (const int fi : face_list) {
    const Eigen::Vector3d c = face_centroid(mesh, fi);
    const Eigen::Vector3d diff = c - fit.center;
    const double axial = diff.dot(fit.axis_direction);
    const Eigen::Vector3d radial_vec = diff - axial * fit.axis_direction;
    const double radial_dist = radial_vec.norm();
    if (radial_dist < 1e-10) continue;
    
    const double dr = radial_dist - fit.major_radius;  // Radial offset from tube center
    const double phi = std::atan2(axial, dr);
    minor_angles.push_back(phi);
}

if (minor_angles.size() < 7) return false;

std::sort(minor_angles.begin(), minor_angles.end());
const int n = static_cast<int>(minor_angles.size());

// Compute largest gap
double largest_gap = minor_angles[0] + 2.0 * M_PI - minor_angles[n - 1];
for (int i = 1; i < n; i++) {
    const double gap = minor_angles[i] - minor_angles[i - 1];
    if (gap > largest_gap) largest_gap = gap;
}

const double span = 2.0 * M_PI - largest_gap;
if (span < 0.1) return false;  // Require meaningful coverage
return true;
```

**Criteria:**
1. Minimum 7 faces
2. Angular span around minor circle >= 0.1 radians
3. Ensures faces wrap around the tube

---

### 6. **CONVEXITY HANDLING**

#### **Determination** (sample for Conical; similar for Spherical/Toroidal)
```cpp
// **Location**: Lines ~484–500 in stage2.cpp

static void determine_convexity(const ConnectedMesh& mesh,
                                const std::vector<int>& face_list,
                                Fit& fit)
{
    int convex_votes = 0;
    for (const int fi : face_list) {
        const Eigen::Vector3d c = face_centroid(mesh, fi);
        const Eigen::Vector3d diff = c - fit.apex;
        const Eigen::Vector3d radial = diff - diff.dot(fit.axis_direction) * fit.axis_direction;
        if (radial.norm() > 1e-10) {
            const double dot = mesh.faces[fi].normal.dot(radial.normalized());
            if (dot > 0) convex_votes++;      // Normal points away from axis
            else convex_votes--;              // Normal points toward axis
        }
    }
    fit.convex = (convex_votes >= 0);  // Majority vote
}
```

**Logic:**
- For cone/cylinder: Normal pointing away from axis = convex (outer surface)
- For sphere: Normal pointing away from center = convex (outer surface)
- For torus: Normal pointing away from tube centerline = convex (outer surface)

#### **Convexity Checking During BFS**
```cpp
// **Location**: Line ~1176 in stage2.cpp

if (!bootstrapping) {
    if (!Traits::convexity_check(current_fit, mesh, ni))
        continue;  // Skip if convexity mismatch
}

// For conical (Lines ~481–492):
static bool convexity_check(const Fit& fit,
                            const ConnectedMesh& mesh, int fi)
{
    const Eigen::Vector3d c = face_centroid(mesh, fi);
    const Eigen::Vector3d diff = c - fit.apex;
    const Eigen::Vector3d radial = diff - diff.dot(fit.axis_direction) * fit.axis_direction;
    const double dot = mesh.faces[fi].normal.dot(radial.normalized());
    // convex: normal points away from axis (positive dot)
    // concave: normal points toward axis (negative dot)
    return fit.convex ? (dot > 0.0) : (dot < 0.0);
}
```

**Timeline:**
1. **Seed phase** (if not bootstrapping): Determine convexity from seed faces
2. **BFS phase**: Check each candidate face against established convexity
3. **After BFS** (if bootstrapping): Determine convexity from final closed set

---

### 7. **WHAT HAPPENS AFTER BFS COMPLETES**

#### **Final Re-fit** (Lines ~1302–1310)
```cpp
// Re-fit surface using ALL accumulated trial faces and vertices
typename Traits::Fit final_fit;
if (Traits::fit_surface(mesh, trial_faces, trial_vertices, final_fit,
                        bootstrapping ? config.verbosity : 0))
    current_fit = final_fit;
else if (bootstrapping && config.verbosity >= 3)
    fprintf(stderr, "    bootstrapped final refit FAILED (%zu faces, %zu verts)\n",
            trial_faces.size(), trial_vertices.size());
```

**Purpose:** 
- Improves fit after adding many faces during BFS
- Uses all trial faces and vertices (not just seed)
- If fails, continues with pre-BFS fit

#### **Bootstrapped Hypothesis Validation** (Lines ~1312–1346)
```cpp
if (bootstrapping) {
    bool valid = true;
    double bootstrap_max_vtol = 0;
    
    // Validate ENTIRE closed set against tolerances
    for (const int tfi : trial_faces) {
        const auto& tf = mesh.faces[tfi];
        for (int i = 0; i < tf.vertex_count; i++) {
            const double d = Traits::vertex_distance(
                current_fit, mesh.vertices[tf.vertex_indices[i]].position);
            if (d > bootstrap_max_vtol) bootstrap_max_vtol = d;
            if (d > vtol) { valid = false; break; }
        }
        if (!valid) break;
        const Eigen::Vector3d tc = face_centroid(mesh, tfi);
        if (Traits::centroid_distance(current_fit, tc) > stol) {
            valid = false; break;
        }
    }
    
    if (!valid) {
        if (config.verbosity >= 3) {
            fprintf(stderr, "    bootstrapped hypothesis vtol fail: max=%.6e (limit %.6e), %zu faces\n",
                    bootstrap_max_vtol, vtol, trial_faces.size());
        }
        acceptance_failures++;
        continue;  // REJECT THIS TRIAL
    }
    
    // Determine convexity from the final fit
    Traits::determine_convexity(mesh, trial_faces, current_fit);
}
```

**Key Points:**
- Only for bootstrapped hypotheses (seed failed initial vtol check)
- Validates complete expanded set against FULL tolerances
- Re-determines convexity from final closed set
- If fails: entire hypothesis is rejected

#### **Acceptance Tests** (Lines ~1348–1350)
```cpp
if (!Traits::acceptance_test(mesh, trial_faces, current_fit, config)) {
    acceptance_failures++;
    continue;  // Reject this trial
}
```

---

### 8. **BEST HYPOTHESIS SELECTION AMONG MULTIPLE SEEDS**

```cpp
// **Location**: Lines ~1352–1360

// Compute total area for best-candidate selection
double total_area = 0;
for (const int tfi : trial_faces)
    total_area += face_area(mesh, mesh.faces[tfi]);

if (total_area > best_area) {
    best_area = total_area;
    best_hypothesis = Traits::make_hypothesis(
        mesh, trial_faces, trial_vertices, current_fit);
}

// After all seeds tried:
if (best_area > 0) {
    committed++;
    const int hyp_idx = static_cast<int>(hypotheses.size());
    hypotheses.push_back(std::move(best_hypothesis));
    for (const int tfi : hypotheses.back().faces) {
        Traits::hypothesis_field(mesh.faces[tfi]) = hyp_idx;
    }
}
```

**Selection Criterion:**
- **Metric**: Total surface area of all trial faces
- **Winner**: Seed that produces largest area hypothesis that passes all tests
- **Commitment**: Only one hypothesis per starting face fi

**Example Scenario (Conical):**
```
Starting face fi generates multiple seeds:
  - Seed 1: {fi, n1, n2} → BFS → 8 faces, area = 100
  - Seed 2: {fi, n3, n4} → BFS → 6 faces, area = 95
  - Seed 3: {fi, n5, n6} → BFS → 12 faces, area = 105  ← WINNER
  
All three pass acceptance tests.
Seed 3 wins because 105 > 100 > 95.
```

I've saved a detailed reference to session memory. Here's what the C++ implementation includes:

**Key Algorithm Highlights:**

1. **Seed Validation is Multi-Stage** (vtol test → stol test → angular test)
2. **Bootstrapping Flag** toggles between strict and relaxed validation modes
3. **"Refit Zone"** is the band where `distance ∈ [tolerance, skip_tolerance]` — this is where expensive fits are attempted
4. **BFS Checks in Order:** Angular → Convexity → Distance Zones (Direct/Skip/Refit)
5. **Post-BFS Validation** happens ONLY for bootstrapped hypotheses
6. **Best Hypothesis** is the one producing maximum total surface area among all seeds
7. **Convexity** is locked after seed phase and cannot change during BFS

The implementation is highly parameterized and uses `Traits` template pattern to share this algorithm across 4 surface types (Planar, Conical, Spherical, Toroidal).

````
### 1. **FULL ALGORITHM PSEUDOCODE**

```pseudocode
────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
TEMPLATE FUNCTION: generate_hypotheses<Traits>()
────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

Input:
  - ConnectedMesh& mesh              (mesh with faces, vertices, connectivity)
  - Stage2Config& config             (tolerances and parameters)
  - std::vector<Hypothesis>& hypotheses  (output: generated hypotheses)

Parameters (from config):
  - vtol           = config.vertex_tolerance          [default: 2e-5]
  - stol           = config.surface_tolerance         [default: 0.4]
  - angular_tol    = config.angular_tolerance_deg     [default: 17.5°]
  - skip_vtol      = vtol * config.refit_skip_multiplier  [= 2e-5 * 10 = 2e-4]
  - skip_stol      = stol * config.refit_skip_multiplier  [= 0.4 * 10 = 4.0]

Algorithm:
────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

FOR EACH face fi IN mesh.faces:
    (skip if already assigned to a hypothesis)
    
    ╔════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╗
    ║ OUTER LOOP: Try all seeds anchored at face fi                         ║
    ╚════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╝
    
    seeds[] = Traits::generate_seeds(mesh, fi, config)
    IF seeds is empty:
        CONTINUE to next face
    
    best_area = -1.0
    best_hypothesis = NULL
    
    FOR EACH seed IN seeds:
        
        ╔════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╗
        ║ PHASE 1: SEED COLLECTION & SURFACE FITTING                            ║
        ╚════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╝
        
        // Collect all unique vertices appearing in seed faces
        seed_vertices[] = EMPTY
        seen_vertices = {}
        FOR EACH sfi IN seed.faces:
            FOR EACH vi IN vertices_of_face(sfi):
                IF vi NOT IN seen_vertices:
                    seed_vertices.append(vi)
                    seen_vertices.insert(vi)
        
        // Fit surface from this seed
        fit = Traits::Fit()
        IF NOT Traits::fit_surface(mesh, seed.faces, seed_vertices, fit):
            fit_failures++
            CONTINUE to next seed  // Fit failed, try next seed
        
        ╔════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╗
        ║ PHASE 1a: SEED-TIME VALIDATION (CRITICAL)                             ║
        ╚════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╝
        
        // ─ TEST 1: Vertex Tolerance at Seed Time ─
        
        seed_valid = TRUE
        seed_max_vtol_error = 0
        
        FOR EACH vi IN seed_vertices:
            dist_i = Traits::vertex_distance(fit, mesh.vertices[vi].position)
            seed_max_vtol_error = MAX(seed_max_vtol_error, dist_i)
            
            IF dist_i > vtol:
                seed_valid = FALSE
                seed_vtol_failures++
                BREAK
        
        // KEY DECISION POINT: Check if bootstrap mode is needed
        bootstrapping = NOT seed_valid
        
        IF bootstrapping:
            // Seed fails vertex tolerance—relax it
            seed_valid = TRUE  // Reset to allow proceeding
        ELSE:
            // ─ TEST 2: Surface (Centroid) Tolerance at Seed Time ─
            // Only do this if not bootstrapping
            
            seed_max_stol_error = 0
            FOR EACH sfi IN seed.faces:
                c = face_centroid(mesh, sfi)
                dist_c = Traits::centroid_distance(fit, c)
                seed_max_stol_error = MAX(seed_max_stol_error, dist_c)
                
                IF dist_c > stol:
                    seed_valid = FALSE
                    seed_stol_failures++
                    BREAK
````

````
### 3. **BFS EXPANSION - EXACT SEQUENCE OF CHECKS**

```cpp
// **Location**: Lines ~1150–1300 in stage2.cpp

std::queue<int> bfs_queue;
for (const int sfi : seed.faces) bfs_queue.push(sfi);

while (!bfs_queue.empty()) {
    const int popped = bfs_queue.front();
    bfs_queue.pop();

                continue;

            const int vi = nf.vertex_indices[i];
            if (!trial_vertex_set.count(vi))
                new_verts.push_back(vi);
````

````
**Exact Sequence Per Candidate Face:**
1. Skip boundary edges and already-assigned faces
2. Check angular tolerance vs neighbors in trial set
3. Check convexity (unless bootstrapping)
4. Compute distances to candidate and its new vertices
5. If distances <= vtol/stol: direct accept
6. If distances > skip_vtol/skip_stol: skip entirely
7. Otherwise: attempt refit with full validation

---

### 4. **THE "REFIT ZONE" / SKIP MULTIPLIER CONCEPT**

```
Distance Zones (for each candidate face ni):

         0
         |
    vtol +────────────── Direct Accept Zone
         |
         |   Refit Zone
         |   (Attempt fit + full validation)
 skip_5  +────────────── Skip Threshold (= vtol * 10.0)
         |
       ∞ +────────────── Too Far (skip entirely)

Parallel for centroid distance:

         0
         |
    stol +────────────── Direct Accept Zone
         |
         |   Refit Zone
         |
 skip_20 +────────────── Skip Threshold (= stol * 10.0)
         |
       ∞ +────────────── Too Far (skip entirely)
```

**Key Code:**
```cpp
// Configuration
const double vtol = config.vertex_tolerance;        // 2e-5 default
const double stol = config.surface_tolerance;       // 0.4 default
const double skip_vtol = vtol * config.refit_skip_multiplier;  // 2e-4
const double skip_stol = stol * config.refit_skip_multiplier;  // 4.0
```

**Decision Logic:**
```cpp
if (max_vtx_dist <= vtol && ctr_dist <= stol) {
    // ZONE 1: Direct accept
    accepted = true;
}
else if (max_vtx_dist > skip_vtol || ctr_dist > skip_stol) {
    // ZONE 3: Too far—skip
    continue;
}
else {
    // ZONE 2: Refit zone—attempt to recover
    // (this is where refit attempts are made)
}
```

**Multiplier Value:**
- Default `refit_skip_multiplier = 10.0` (line ~62 in stage2.h)
- Creates a 10x tolerance band around normal tolerance
- Balances computational cost with expansion opportunities

---

### 5. **ACCEPTANCE TEST LOGIC (Surface-Type Specific)**

#### **Planar** (Lines ~239–248)
```cpp
static bool acceptance_test(const ConnectedMesh& /*mesh*/,
                            const std::vector<int>& face_list,
                            const Fit& /*fit*/,
                            const Stage2Config& /*config*/)
{
    // Single-face hypotheses are not kept
    return face_list.size() >= 2;
}
```
- **Criterion**: Minimum 2 faces
- **Rationale**: Single-face planar is trivial, needs at least one neighbor

#### **Conical/Cylindrical** (Lines ~537–574)
```cpp
if (face_list.size() < 3) return false;  // Min 3 faces

// Angular coverage validation
// Build orthonormal basis perpendicular to axis
Eigen::Vector3d u, w;
{
    Eigen::Vector3d arbitrary = (std::abs(fit.axis_direction.x()) < 0.9)
        ? Eigen::Vector3d(1, 0, 0) : Eigen::Vector3d(0, 1, 0);
    u = fit.axis_direction.cross(arbitrary).normalized();
    w = fit.axis_direction.cross(u).normalized();
}

// Compute azimuthal angles
std::vector<double> thetas;
thetas.reserve(face_list.size());
for (const int fi : face_list) {
    const Eigen::Vector3d c = face_centroid(mesh, fi);
    const Eigen::Vector3d diff = c - fit.apex;
    const Eigen::Vector3d radial = diff - diff.dot(fit.axis_direction) * fit.axis_direction;
    const double theta = std::atan2(radial.dot(w), radial.dot(u));
    thetas.push_back(theta);
}

std::sort(thetas.begin(), thetas.end());
const int n = static_cast<int>(thetas.size());
if (n < 3) return false;

// Compute gaps between consecutive angles
std::vector<double> gaps;
gaps.reserve(n);
for (int i = 1; i < n; i++) {
    gaps.push_back(thetas[i] - thetas[i - 1]);
}
gaps.push_back(thetas[0] + 2.0 * M_PI - thetas[n - 1]);  // Wrapar
````

````
3. Prevents accepting fillet-like (1D/2D) faces

#### **Toroidal** (Lines ~1022–1062)

if (face_list.size() < 7) return false;

// Angular coverage around minor circle (tube cross-section)
std::vector<double> minor_angles;
minor_angles.reserve(face_list.size());
for (const int fi : face_list) {
    const Eigen::Vector3d c = face_centroid(mesh, fi);
    const Eigen::Vector3d diff = c - fit.center;
    const double axial = diff.dot(fit.axis_direction);
    const Eigen::Vector3d radial_vec = diff - axial * fit.axis_direction;
    const double radial_dist = radial_vec.norm();
    if (radial_dist < 1e-10) continue;
    
    const double dr = radial_dist - fit.major_radius;  // Radial offset from tube center
    const double phi = std::atan2(axial, dr);
    minor_angles.push_back(phi);
}

if (minor_angles.size() < 7) return false;

std::sort(minor_angles.begin(), minor_angles.end());
const int n = static_cast<int>(minor_angles.size());

// Compute largest gap
double largest_gap = minor_angles[0] + 2.0 * M_PI - minor_angles[n - 1];
for (int i = 1; i < n; i++) {
    const double gap = minor_angles[i] - minor_angles[i - 1];
    if (gap > largest_gap) largest_gap = gap;
}

const double span = 2.0 * M_PI - largest_gap;
if (span < 0.1) return false;  // Require meaningful coverage
return true;
```

**Criteria:**
1. Minimum 7 faces
2. Angular span around minor circle >= 0.1 radians
3. Ensures faces wrap around the tube

### 6. **CONVEXITY HANDLING**

#### **Determination** (sample for Conical; similar for Spherical/Toroidal)
```cpp
// **Location**: Lines ~484–500 in stage2.cpp

static void determine_convexity(const ConnectedMesh& mesh,
                                const std::vector<int>& face_list,
                                Fit& fit)
{
    int convex_votes = 0;
    for (const int fi : face_list) {
        const Eigen::Vector3d c = face_centroid(mesh, fi);
        const Eigen::Vector3d diff = c - fit.apex;
        const Eigen::Vector3d radial = diff - diff.dot(fit.axis_direction) * fit.axis_direction;
        if (radial.norm() > 1e-10) {
            const double dot = mesh.faces[fi].normal.dot(radial.normalized());
            if (dot > 0) convex_votes++;      // Normal points away from axis
            else convex_votes--;              // Normal points toward axis
        }
    }
    fit.convex = (convex_votes >= 0);  // Majority vote
}
```

**Logic:**
- For cone/cylinder: Normal pointing away from axis = convex (outer surface)
- For sphere: Normal pointing away from center = convex (outer surface)
- For torus: Normal pointing away from tube centerline = convex (outer surface)

#### **Convexity Checking During BFS**
```cpp
// **Location**: Line ~1176 in stage2.cpp

if (!bootstrapping) {
    if (!Traits::convexity_check(current_fit, mesh, ni))
        continue;  // Skip if convexity mismatch
}

// For conical (Lines ~481–492):
static bool convexity_check(const Fit& fit,
                            const ConnectedMesh& mesh, int fi)
{
    const Eigen::Vector3d c = face_centroid(mesh, fi);
    const Eigen::Vector3d diff = c - fit.apex;
    const Eigen::Vector3d radial = diff - diff.dot(fit.axis_direction) * fit.axis_direction;
    const double dot = mesh.faces[fi].normal.dot(radial.normalized());
    // convex: normal points away from axis (positive dot)
    // concave: normal points toward axis (negative dot)
    return fit.convex ? (dot > 0.0) : (dot < 0.0);
}
```
````

````
        if (config.verbosity >= 3) {
            fprintf(stderr, "    bootstrapped hypothesis vtol fail: max=%.6e (limit %.6e), %zu faces\n",
                    bootstrap_max_vtol, vtol, trial_faces.size());
        }
        acceptance_failures++;
        continue;  // REJECT THIS TRIAL
    }
    
    // Determine convexity from the final fit
    Traits::determine_convexity(mesh, trial_faces, current_fit);
}
```

**Key Points:**
- Only for bootstrapped hypotheses (seed failed initial vtol check)
- Validates complete expanded set against FULL tolerances
- Re-determines convexity from final closed set
- If fails: entire hypothesis is rejected

### 8. **BEST HYPOTHESIS SELECTION AMONG MULTIPLE SEEDS**

```cpp
// **Location**: Lines ~1352–1360

// Compute total area for best-candidate selection
double total_area = 0;
for (const int tfi : trial_faces)
    total_area += face_area(mesh, mesh.faces[tfi]);

if (total_area > best_area) {
    best_area = total_area;
    best_hypothesis = Traits::make_hypothesis(
        mesh, trial_faces, trial_vertices, current_fit);
}

// After all seeds tried:
if (best_area > 0) {
    committed++;
    const int hyp_idx = static_cast<int>(hypotheses.size());
    hypotheses.push_back(std::move(best_hypothesis));
    for (const int tfi : hypotheses.back().faces) {
        Traits::hypothesis_field(mesh.faces[tfi]) = hyp_idx;
    }
}
```

**Selection Criterion:**
- **Metric**: Total surface area of all trial faces
- **Winner**: Seed that produces largest area hypothesis that passes all tests
- **Commitment**: Only one hypothesis per starting face fi

**Example Scenario (Conical):**
```
Starting face fi generates multiple seeds:
  - Seed 1: {fi, n1, n2} → BFS → 8 faces, area = 100
  - Seed 2: {fi, n3, n4} → BFS → 6 faces, area = 95
  - Seed 3: {fi, n5, n6} → BFS → 12 faces, area = 105  ← WINNER
  
All three pass acceptance tests.
Seed 3 wins because 105 > 100 > 95.
```
````

```
//! Generic BFS hypothesis generation framework.
//!
//! Provides a trait-based abstraction for surface hypothesis BFS, parameterized
//! by surface type. Each surface type (planar, conical, spherical, toroidal)
//! implements the `SurfaceType` trait, and the shared `generate_hypotheses()`
//! function handles the BFS expansion loop.
//!
//! Ported from the C++ `generate_hypotheses<Traits>()` template in brepper-cpp2.

use std::collections::{HashSet, VecDeque};

use crate::stage1::{ConnectedMesh, MeshFace, NO_HYPOTHESIS};
use crate::viz::VizSender;

use super::{dot3, face_area, face_centroid, REFIT_SKIP_MULTIPLIER};

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// BFS configuration
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

/// Configuration for the BFS hypothesis generation.
pub(super) struct BfsConfig {
    pub vertex_tol: f64,
    pub surface_tol: f64,
    pub angular_tol: f64,
    pub verbosity: u8,
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Surface type trait
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

/// A seed for BFS expansion: a set of face indices to start from.
pub(super) struct Seed {
    pub faces: Vec<usize>,
}

/// Trait that each surface type implements to parameterize the BFS.
///
/// The generic `generate_hypotheses()` function calls these methods to:
/// - Generate seed face sets for each starting face
/// - Fit a surface to a set of faces/vertices
/// - Compute vertex and centroid distances to the fitted surface
/// - Check convexity consistency
/// - Validate angular coverage and other acceptance criteria
/// - Construct the final hypothesis struct
pub(super) trait SurfaceType {
    /// The fitted surface parameters (e.g., axis, radius, center).
    type Fit: Clone;
    /// The output hypothesis struct.
```

```
    type Hypothesis;

    /// Human-readable name for logging (e.g., "planar", "conical", "spherical").
    fn name() -> &'static str;

    /// Access the hypothesis index field on a MeshFace (read).
    fn hypothesis_field(face: &MeshFace) -> i32;

    /// Access the hypothesis index field on a MeshFace (write).
    fn set_hypothesis_field(face: &mut MeshFace, value: i32);

    /// The "undeduced" sentinel value for this surface type's hypothesis field.
    fn undeduced_value() -> i32;

    /// Check whether a face is eligible for BFS expansion (not already claimed
    /// by a prior hypothesis or excluded by other surface types).
    fn face_eligible(face: &MeshFace) -> bool {
        Self::hypothesis_field(face) == Self::undeduced_value()
    }

    /// Generate seed face sets anchored at face `fi`.
    /// Returns an empty vec if `fi` is not a valid seed anchor.
    fn generate_seeds(mesh: &ConnectedMesh, fi: usize, config: &BfsConfig) -> Vec<Seed>;

    /// Fit a surface to the given faces and vertices.
    /// Returns None if fitting fails.
    fn fit_surface(
        mesh: &ConnectedMesh,
        face_list: &[usize],
        vertex_list: &[usize],
        verbosity: u8,
    ) -> Option<Self::Fit>;

    /// Compute the absolute distance from a vertex position to the fitted surface.
    fn vertex_distance(fit: &Self::Fit, pos: &[f64; 3]) -> f64;

    /// Compute the absolute distance from a centroid to the fitted surface.
    /// Default: same as vertex_distance.
    fn centroid_distance(fit: &Self::Fit, centroid: &[f64; 3]) -> f64 {
        Self::vertex_distance(fit, centroid)
    }

    /// Check convexity consistency: does `fi` match the convexity of the fit?
    /// Return true to accept, false to reject.
    /// Default implementation always accepts (e.g., for planar surfaces).
    fn convexity_check(_fit: &Self::Fit, _mesh: &ConnectedMesh, _fi: usize) -> bool {
        true
    }

    /// Determine convexity from a set of faces and update the fit.
    /// Default: no-op (for surface types without convexity).
    fn determine_convexity(_mesh: &ConnectedMesh, _face_list: &[usize], _fit: &mut Self::Fit) {}

    /// Post-BFS acceptance test (e.g., angular coverage, minimum face count).
    /// Return true to accept the hypothesis.
    fn acceptance_test(
        mesh: &ConnectedMesh,
        face_list: &[usize],
        fit: &Self::Fit,
        config: &BfsConfig,
    ) -> bool;

    /// Multiplier for seed vertex tolerance.  Defaults to 1.0.
    /// Cone seeds are inherently noisier (mixed-face apex neighborhoods)
    /// so they override this to allow a looser seed threshold.
    fn seed_vtol_multiplier() -> f64 { 1.0 }

    /// Multiplier for the angular tolerance used in dihedral-angle checks.
    /// Defaults to 1.0.  Cones override to 2.0 because tessellated cone tips
    /// have inter-strip dihedral angles that exceed the default tolerance.
    fn angular_tol_multiplier() -> f64 { 1.0 }

    /// Construct the final hypothesis from the BFS result.
    fn make_hypothesis(
        mesh: &ConnectedMesh,
        faces: Vec<usize>,
        vertices: Vec<usize>,
        fit: &Self::Fit,
    ) -> Self::Hypothesis;
}
```

```
///
/// Iterates through all faces, generating seeds for each eligible face,
/// running trial BFS expansions, and committing the best hypothesis per
/// starting face. Returns the list of accepted hypotheses and whether
/// viz was quit.
pub(super) fn generate_hypotheses<S>(
    mesh: &mut ConnectedMesh,
    config: &BfsConfig,
    _viz: Option<&VizSender>,
) -> (Vec<S::Hypothesis>, bool)
where
    S: SurfaceType,
    S::Hypothesis: HypothesisFaces,
{
    let num_faces = mesh.faces.len();
    let vtol = config.vertex_tol;
    let stol = config.surface_tol;
    let skip_vtol = vtol * REFIT_SKIP_MULTIPLIER;
    let skip_stol = stol * REFIT_SKIP_MULTIPLIER;
    let angular_tol = config.angular_tol * S::angular_tol_multiplier();

    let mut hypotheses: Vec<S::Hypothesis> = Vec::new();

    // Statistics
    let mut seeds_generated = 0_u32;
    let mut seeds_tried = 0_u32;
    let mut fit_failures = 0_u32;
    let mut seed_stol_failures = 0_u32;
    let mut seed_angle_failures = 0_u32;
    let mut acceptance_failures = 0_u32;
    let mut committed = 0_u32;

    // PROFILING: timing instrumentation
    let prof_start = std::time::Instant::now();
    let mut prof_faces_visited = 0_u64;
    let mut prof_total_seed_count = 0_u64;
    let mut prof_total_bfs_faces = 0_u64;
    let mut prof_total_fit_calls = 0_u64;
    let mut prof_total_refit_calls = 0_u64;
    let mut prof_time_seed_gen = std::time::Duration::ZERO;
    let mut prof_time_seed_fit = std::time::Duration::ZERO;
    let mut prof_time_bfs = std::time::Duration::ZERO;
    let mut prof_time_refit = std::time::Duration::ZERO;
    let mut prof_time_final_fit = std::time::Duration::ZERO;
    let mut prof_time_acceptance = std::time::Duration::ZERO;
    let mut prof_max_seeds_per_face = 0_usize;
    let mut prof_max_bfs_faces = 0_usize;
    let mut prof_last_report = std::time::Instant::now();

    // Initialize hypothesis fields to UNDEDUCED
    for fi in 0..num_faces {
        S::set_hypothesis_field(&mut mesh.faces[fi], S::undeduced_value());
    }

    for fi in 0..num_faces {
        if !S::face_eligible(&mesh.faces[fi]) {
            continue;
        }

        prof_faces_visited += 1;
        // PROFILING: periodic progress report every 2 seconds
        if prof_last_report.elapsed().as_secs_f64() > 2.0 {
            eprintln!(
                "  [{}-BFS PROFILE] fi={}/{} ({:.1}%), elapsed={:.1}s, seeds_so_far={}, fits={}, refits={}, committed={}",
                S::name(), fi, num_faces, 100.0 * fi as f64 / num_faces as f64,
                prof_start.elapsed().as_secs_f64(),
                prof_total_seed_count, prof_total_fit_calls, prof_total_refit_calls, committed,
            );
            prof_last_report = std::time::Instant::now();
        }

        let t_seed_gen = std::time::Instant::now();
        let seeds = S::generate_seeds(mesh, fi, config);
        prof_time_seed_gen += t_seed_gen.elapsed();
        if seeds.is_empty() {
            continue;
        }
        prof_total_seed_count += seeds.len() as u64;
        if seeds.len() > prof_max_seeds_per_face {
            prof_max_seeds_per_face = seeds.len();
        }
```

```
///
/// Iterates through all faces, generating seeds for each eligible face,
/// running trial BFS expansions, and committing the best hypothesis per
/// starting face. Returns the list of accepted hypotheses and whether
/// viz was quit.
pub(super) fn generate_hypotheses<S>(
    mesh: &mut ConnectedMesh,
    config: &BfsConfig,
    _viz: Option<&VizSender>,
) -> (Vec<S::Hypothesis>, bool)
where
    S: SurfaceType,
    S::Hypothesis: HypothesisFaces,
{
    let num_faces = mesh.faces.len();
    let vtol = config.vertex_tol;
    let stol = config.surface_tol;
    let skip_vtol = vtol * REFIT_SKIP_MULTIPLIER;
    let skip_stol = stol * REFIT_SKIP_MULTIPLIER;
    let angular_tol = config.angular_tol * S::angular_tol_multiplier();

    let mut hypotheses: Vec<S::Hypothesis> = Vec::new();

    // Statistics
    let mut seeds_generated = 0_u32;
    let mut seeds_tried = 0_u32;
    let mut fit_failures = 0_u32;
    let mut seed_stol_failures = 0_u32;
    let mut seed_angle_failures = 0_u32;
    let mut acceptance_failures = 0_u32;
    let mut committed = 0_u32;

    // PROFILING: timing instrumentation
    let prof_start = std::time::Instant::now();
    let mut prof_faces_visited = 0_u64;
    let mut prof_total_seed_count = 0_u64;
    let mut prof_total_bfs_faces = 0_u64;
    let mut prof_total_fit_calls = 0_u64;
    let mut prof_total_refit_calls = 0_u64;
    let mut prof_time_seed_gen = std::time::Duration::ZERO;
    let mut prof_time_seed_fit = std::time::Duration::ZERO;
    let mut prof_time_bfs = std::time::Duration::ZERO;
    let mut prof_time_refit = std::time::Duration::ZERO;
    let mut prof_time_final_fit = std::time::Duration::ZERO;
    let mut prof_time_acceptance = std::time::Duration::ZERO;
    let mut prof_max_seeds_per_face = 0_usize;
    let mut prof_max_bfs_faces = 0_usize;
    let mut prof_last_report = std::time::Instant::now();

    // Initialize hypothesis fields to UNDEDUCED
    for fi in 0..num_faces {
        S::set_hypothesis_field(&mut mesh.faces[fi], S::undeduced_value());
```
